## Review

**6 out of 10, Needs substantial work.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

This is a disciplined, consistently branded investor reference deck with clean tables, readable headers and a coherent section route (snapshot, strategy, credit, capital, funding), but it is mostly data pages with topic titles rather than message titles. Slides 27, 29, 30 and 31 give one thin highlight bullet beside large tables, so the audience must find the point themselves, and many pages (25, 26, 34, 39, 41, 43) are very small text or dense blocks that are hard to read live. Slides 7 and 12 are well organised and legible, yet slide 12 mixes eight metrics, four updates and six targets with little emphasis on what matters most. It works as a read-ahead document for analysts, but substantial revision would be needed for it to communicate a case on screen.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent green header and footer system, the clean banded tables on slides 27 and 30, and the three-pillar structure with targets strip on slides 7 and 8.

**Most useful repair:** Rewrite topic titles as takeaway statements and give each table or chart slide a stated conclusion, especially slides 27 to 31 where one short bullet sits beside a large table.

**What would change the score:** I would split or trim the densest pages (slides 12, 36, 39, 41, 43) and move the long forward-looking text on slide 2 and the endnotes on slides 49 to 54 into a handout, with the first slide's empty lower area and the sparse slide 55 tidied.

_Confidence medium. 55 of 55 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

This is TD Bank Group's Q1 2026 investor presentation, opening with a forward-looking statements caution and a title slide, then a snapshot of the bank's businesses, national ranking and the Q1 2026 performance figures. The middle sections cover the four business lines and their F'26 strategic priorities, cost reduction and restructuring, the U.S. BSA/AML remediation, AI initiatives and digital engagement. Credit is the largest block, with allowance and impairment trends, provision ratios by segment, and detail on Canadian residential real estate lending, commercial lending and U.S. personal and commercial portfolios. Later sections address capital and CET1 movements, TLAC, liquidity, funding, credit ratings, covered bonds, bail-in and NVCC loss absorption, and interest rate sensitivity. The appendix covers economic outlook and interest rate views, followed by endnotes defining non-GAAP measures and footnotes for each slide. The deck is built as a dense, data-heavy sequence of tables and charts with a consistent slide header and repeated forward-looking disclaimers.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the TD Bank Group Q1 2026 investor presentation have?**

The deck has 55 pages. Slide 3 notes that explanatory endnotes are included on Slides 48-53.

**What Q1 2026 net income and EPS does TD Bank Group report in its investor presentation?**

Slide 12 shows Q1 2026 net income of $4.0B reported and $4.2B adjusted, and EPS of $2.34 reported and $2.44 adjusted.

**What CET1 ratio does TD Bank Group report for Q1 2026?**

Slide 5 lists a Common Equity Tier 1 capital ratio of 14.5%, ranked 1st in Canada and 1st in North America. Slide 12 also reports a CET1 ratio of 14.5% for Q1 2026.

**What cost reduction targets does TD Bank Group outline in its investor presentation?**

Slide 13 states that the bank is targeting about $2-2.5B in annualized cost savings over the medium-term, and expects F'26 savings of about $900MM pre-tax. It also notes a completed restructuring program with total charges of $886MM pre-tax.

Forward-looking statements are typically identified by words such as “will”, “would”, “should”, “suggest”, “seek”, “believe”, “expect”, “anticipate”, “intend”, “ambition”, “strive”, “confident”, “estimate”, “forecast”, “outlook”, “plan”, “goal”, “commit” “target”, “"objective", "timeline", possible”, “potential”, “predict”, “project”, “foresee”, “may”, and “could” and similar expressions or variations thereof, or the negative thereof, but these terms are not the exclusive means of identifying such statements. Examples of such risk factors include: general business and economic conditions in the regions in which the Bank operates; geopolitical risk (including policy, trade and tax-related risks and the potential impact of any new or elevated tariffs or any retaliatory tariffs); inflation, interest rates and recession uncertainty; risks associated with the remediation of the Bank's U.S. Bank Secrecy Act (BSA)/anti-money laundering (AML) program and Enterprise AML program; regulatory oversight and compliance risk; the ability of the Bank to execute on long-term strategies, shorter-term key strategic priorities, including the successful completion of acquisitions and dispositions and integration of acquisitions, the ability of the Bank to achieve its financial or strategic objectives with respect to its investments, business retention plans, and other strategic plans; risks associated with the insured deposit account agreement between the Bank and The Charles Schwab Corporation; technology and cyber security risk (including cyber-attacks, data security breaches or technology failures) on the Bank’s technologies, systems and networks, those of the Bank’s customers (including their own devices), and third parties providing services to the Bank; data risk; model risk; external fraud activity; insider risk; conduct risk; the failure of third parties to comply with their obligations to the Bank or its affiliates, including relating to the care and control of information, and other risks arising from the Bank’s use of third-parties; the impact of new and changes to, or application of, current laws, rules and regulations, including consumer protection laws and regulations, tax laws, capital guidelines and liquidity regulatory guidance; environmental and social risk (including climate-related risk); exposure related to litigation and regulatory matters; increased competition from incumbents and new entrants (including Fintechs and big technology competitors); shifts in consumer attitudes and disruptive technology; ability of the Bank to attract, develop, and retain key talent; changes in foreign exchange rates, interest rates, credit spreads, equity prices and commodity prices; downgrade, suspension or withdrawal of ratings assigned by any rating agency, the value and market price of the Bank's common shares and other securities may be impacted by market conditions and other factors; the interconnectivity of financial institutions, including existing and potential international debt crises; increased funding costs and market volatility due to market illiquidity and competition for funding; and critical accounting estimates and changes to accounting standards, policies, and methods used by the Bank; and the occurrence of natural and unnatural catastrophic events and claims resulting from such events. The Bank cautions that the preceding list is not exhaustive of all possible risk factors and other factors could also adversely affect the Bank’s results. For more detailed information, please refer to the “Risk Factors that May Affect Future Results” section of the 2025 MD&A, and the sections related to strategic, credit, market (including equity, commodity, foreign exchange, interest rate, and credit spreads), operational (including technology, cyber security, process, systems, data, third-party, fraud, infrastructure, insider and conduct), model, insurance, liquidity, capital adequacy, compliance, financial crime, reputational, environmental and social risk in the "Managing Risk" section of the 2025 MD&A, as may be updated in subsequently filed quarterly reports to shareholders and news releases (as applicable) related to any events or transactions discussed under the headings “Significant Events” or “Update on the Remediation of the U.S. Bank Secrecy Act (BSA)/Anti-Money Laundering (AML) Program and Enterprise AML Program” in the relevant MD&A, which applicable releases may be found on www.td.com. ### Slide 3

**Text**

TD Bank Group / Overview of TD Bank Group / Credit Update / Strong Balance Sheet & Capital Position / NB: Explanatory endnotes are included on Slides 48-53. / 2 / — TD INVESTOR PRESENTATION Q1 2026

**Layout**

Two-zone split layout. A full-height photograph occupies the left 60.1% of the slide, and a white panel occupies the right side. The title 'TD Bank Group' sits top-left of the right panel at about 10% from the top, with the TD logo square in the top-right corner. Three stacked horizontal light-green bands start at x 42.7% and run to the right edge, with equal 15.3% heights and 2.5% vertical gaps, vertically centred in the lower-middle area. Each band has a chevron at its left and its label left-aligned at x 50.6%. The endnote sits bottom-right, aligned to x 61.8%. The footer (page number and label) sits bottom-left over the photograph, at about 92.8% from the top.

**Visuals**

One photograph and one logo, plus three chevron icons. Photograph: a low-angle view looking up at a modern building facade. A large green-tinted glass and metal corner panel carries a cream-white TD wordmark on a raised angled sign. Above it, grey-silver aluminium and dark glass window bands rise into a pale blue sky with white clouds at the top-left. The dark tower on the upper right is in shadow. Lighting is soft daylight. Dominant colours are green (about #1E7A32), silver-grey, and pale sky blue. The image is cropped at the panel edge and is not full-bleed across the slide, with no overlay or tint. Logo: the green square TD logo with a white TD wordmark, top-right. Chevrons: three thin right-pointing chevron strokes, one in each band. The first is dark green #03522C, and the other two are light green #B1D57F.

**Design**

Background is white #FFFFFF. Title 'TD Bank Group' is in Arial Bold, green #008A00, about 5.2% of slide height, with no capitals or letter-spacing. Active band label 'Overview of TD Bank Group' is Arial Bold, dark green #03522C, about 4.4% of slide height. Inactive band labels are Arial Regular, light green #B1D57F, about 4.4% of slide height. Bands are flat #E5F1D4 rectangles with no border, no corner radius, no shadow and no gradient. Endnote is Arial Italic, grey #A6A6A6, about 1.7%. Footer is Arial Regular, white #FFFFFF, about 1.5%. Chevrons are thin stroked lines, about 2px, with no fill.

**Visuals**

Four solid dark green header bars (#006700) stacked down the left column, each carrying a white bold heading with a green square-bullet list beneath. On the right: (1) an illustration of a light grey North America map with province and state borders in thin mid-grey lines and small green dots (#33B233) marking retail locations, concentrated in Ontario, Quebec, the Prairies and the US Northeast and Southeast; a few dark dots mark TD Wealth points. (3) A pale green caption box reading 2,092 retail locations in North America. (4) An illustration of a light grey world map with green dots (TD Securities) clustered over North America, Western Europe and East Asia and dark grey dots (TD Wealth) over North America and Japan; a small legend at bottom-left of the map reads Legend, TD Securities (green dot), TD Wealth (dark dot). (5) A pale green bottom caption box spanning the lower right with two centred lines of text. No charts or photographs.

**Layout**

Single slide with a left-aligned title at the top-left and one full-width data table below it, spanning roughly x 3.5% to 96.5% and y 22% to 69% of the slide. The table has a green header row, then seven body rows of equal height (about 6.5% each), with four columns: a wide label column, a shaded TD Bank Group column, and two narrow ranking columns. The TD logo sits in the top-right corner. The footer line (slide number and document title) sits bottom-left at about y 92.7%. Left margin is about 3.5% and right margin about 3.5%. Nothing is in the lower third except the footer.

**Visuals**

One data table, no chart, photograph or diagram. Columns: (1) row label, blank header cell beneath 'Q1 2026 (C$ except otherwise noted)'; (2) 'TD Bank Group'; (3) 'Canadian Ranking¹'; (4) 'North American Ranking²'. Rows, with values as printed: Total assets | $2,099B | 2nd | 6th. Total deposits | $1,245B | 2nd | 6th. Market capitalization | $212.7B | 2nd | 6th. Reported net income (trailing four quarters) | $21.8B | 1st | 4th. Adjusted net income³ (trailing four quarters) | $15.6B | n/a | n/a. Average number of full-time equivalent staff | 104,841 | 1st | 5th. Common Equity Tier 1 capital ratio⁴ | 14.5% | 1st | 1st. Ordinal suffixes (nd, st, th) are printed as small superscripts. Highlights: the TD Bank Group column is shaded light grey on alternate cells; no cell is coloured beyond that. The TD logo (green square with white 'TD' letters) is the only other visual element.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background, no gradients or shadows. Title in bold green (#008A00) sans-serif (Arial/Helvetica Bold), about 5.2% of slide height, not italic, sentence-case. Table header band in solid dark green #006700 with bold white sans-serif text about 2.6% of slide height; the header caption 'Q1 2026' is bold and '(C$ except otherwise noted)' is regular weight. Row labels in bold dark green #03522C, about 2.6% of height; the parenthetical notes are bold italic in the same green. Values in regular weight near-black #1C1C1C, about 2.6% of height. Table rules are thin light grey lines (#D9D9D9-like) separating rows; cell corners are square; no rounded corners. Shaded column fill #F2F2F2 behind the TD Bank Group values on the body rows. Footer text in regular black sans-serif about 1.5% of height, with page number in #1C1C1C.

### Slide 6

**Text**

Scaled, Diversified North American Bank / Four key business lines / Canadian Personal & Commercial Banking / The Personal Bank is a premier retail banking franchise delivering personalized / solutions through a full suite of products and services across deposits, investing, / payments and lending. / Business Banking is a premier, client-centric franchise that delivers deep sector / expertise, valuable advice, and a broad range of products and services to meet / the needs of business owners. / U.S. Banking / Includes the Bank's personal, business banking and wealth management / operations in the U.S. / Wealth Management & Insurance / Wealth Management offers wealth solutions to retail clients in Canada through the / self-directed brokerage, advice-based, and asset management businesses. / Insurance offers property and casualty insurance through direct channels and to / members of affinity groups, as well as life and health insurance products to / customers across Canada. / Wholesale Banking / Operating under the brand names TD Securities and TD Cowen, Wholesale / Banking offers global markets and corporate and investment banking services to / its corporate, government, and institutional clients. / Q1 2026 Reported Earnings Mix 1 / Canadian / Personal & / Commercial / Banking, / 46% / U.S. Banking, / 24% / Wealth / Management / & Insurance, / 17% / Wholesale / Banking, 13% / 5 — TD INVESTOR PRESENTATION Q1 2026

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white page: the title sits top-left at about 10% from the top, spanning roughly 58% of the width, with a left text column (x 3.6% to about 56%) holding the subtitle and four green section headings, each followed by bullets, and a right column (x about 55% to 93%) holding the chart heading and donut chart. A thin vertical grey divider runs at about 53% of the width from about 20% to 94% of the height separating the columns. A TD logo sits in the top-right corner, and the footer line with page number 5 sits bottom-left at about 93% from the top, left-aligned at a margin of about 4%.

**Visuals**

Chart: donut chart titled 'Q1 2026 Reported Earnings Mix' with superscript footnote marker 1, showing four segments of reported earnings share, with percentage labels printed on the segments (no axes, no legend, no gridlines). Segments clockwise from top: Canadian Personal & Commercial Banking 46% (fill #008A00, dark-medium green, the largest arc on the left and top-left, spanning roughly from 12 o'clock counter-clockwise to about 5 o'clock); U.S. Banking 24% (fill #163D22, very dark green, upper-right arc from 12 o'clock to about 3 o'clock); Wealth Management & Insurance 17% (fill #AED99E, pale green, right-lower arc); Wholesale Banking 13% (fill #86CA72, medium-light green, bottom arc). Labels are white bold text on the two dark segments (Canadian and U.S. Banking) and dark #1C1C1C bold text on the two light segments (Wealth Management and Wholesale). Donut has a white hole at about 50% of the outer diameter and thin white separator lines between segments. No highlighted points. Logo: TD logo, a green square (#00A000 approx.) with a white stylised 'TD' mark, at the top-right corner, not cropped.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient, texture or imagery. Title in Arial Bold, dark-medium green #008A00, about 5.2% of slide height, sentence case. Chart heading in Arial Bold, dark green #03522C, about 4.4% of slide height. Section headings in Arial Bold, green #008A00, about 2.6% of slide height. Subtitle 'Four key business lines' in Arial Bold, #006600, about 3.7%. Body text in Arial regular, dark green #03522C, about 2.2% of slide height, with bullets in Wingdings square glyph in #03522C. Footer and page number in Arial regular, about 1.5%. Vertical divider in mid grey #A0A0A0, about 0.1% width. No corner radii, shadows or gradients; chart segments are flat fills with thin white borders.

### Slide 7

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A three-column layout pairs each theme with a short result line and a large medium-term target panel, so the strategy and the numbers read together at a glance.
- Review: Slides 7 and 12 are well organised and legible, yet slide 12 mixes eight metrics, four updates and six targets with little emphasis on what matters most.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent green header and footer system, the clean banded tables on slides 27 and 30, and the three-pillar structure with targets strip on slides 7 and 8.

This Slide contains forward-looking information. Please refer to the caution at the beginning of this presentation and the endnotes for this slide for material factors, risks and assumptions.

**Layout**

Three equal-width cards sit in a row across the upper half, each with a bold heading and a green underline bar, followed by a full-width medium-term targets band of eight columns separated by thin vertical rules. The title sits top-left at about 9.5% from the top with a 5.2% left margin; the TD logo is top-right. The footer carries the page number at left, the footer text and a forward-looking disclaimer below, all left-aligned to about a 5% margin.

**Visuals**

A small TD logo in the top-right corner: a green square with white 'TD' letters, roughly 42 by 37 pixels. No photographs or charts. The medium-term targets band is a row of eight text-based metrics divided by thin grey vertical rules, not a chart. Column values: ~16% over Adj² ROE; 7-10% over Adj² EPS¹ Growth; Mid-to-high single digits over Adj² PTPP⁴ CAGR¹ (%); Positive over Adj² Operating Leverage⁵; Mid-50s over Adj² Efficiency Ratio (Net of ISE, %)¹˒⁶; Strong over CET1 Ratio; 40-50% over Dividend Payout Ratio¹; 75+ bps over Annual Adj² Organic Capital Accretion⁷. The three green underline bars under the card headings are horizontal lines, about 0.5% of slide height thick, rounded ends.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background with light green pale panels. Title is Arial Bold (sans-serif, bold, about 5.2% of slide height) in green #008A00. Card headings are Arial Bold about 3.3% of slide height in dark green #007400. Body copy is Arial regular about 2.6% of slide height in dark green #03522C. Target figures are Arial Bold about 3.3% in #007400 and their descriptors Arial regular 2.6% in #03522C. Accent bars and the Medium-term rule are solid #007400 lines. Card panels are square-cornered with no shadows and no borders. Footer text is Arial about 1.5% in black, disclaimer italic Arial about 1.7% in grey #A6A6A6. Superscript footnote markers are small raised digits about 1.7% high.

**Review notes**

**Text**

Canadian P&C Banking – F'26 Strategic Priorities
Deeper Relationships
Simpler & Faster
Disciplined Execution
Outgrow Canadian population growth in net new clients and lead in core banking and primacy
Deepen client relationships across CPB, CBB, and Wealth Management
Transform RESL homebuying and retention through specialization and speed while delivering strong returns
Accelerate Credit Card growth by streamlining processes, and modernizing policies and technology
Expand CBB distribution by investing in frontline bankers and focusing on relationship banking
Modernize and automate processes and platforms, and leverage AI to deliver efficient, seamless, and personalized experiences
Deliver simpler and faster client experiences for onboarding and mobile first experiences
Leverage AI and automation to enhance auto-adjudication rates in TD Auto Finance and Small Business Banking, while streamlining CBB credit processes
Leverage automation, digital tools and AI-at-scale to drive cost savings and improve efficiency
Redesign key business processes and streamline operations to realize cost reduction
Deliver disciplined expense management through resource optimization and third-party spend reduction
Drive distribution transformation to meet clients' evolving needs and create reinvestment opportunities
7
— TD INVESTOR PRESENTATION Q1 2026

**Layout**

Three equal-width columns of tinted panels sit side by side below a single left-aligned title at the top left, with the TD logo in the top-right corner. Each column has a bold green heading with a green rule beneath it, followed by left-aligned body paragraphs; a small footer with page number and label sits at bottom left, on a white background with generous margins.

**Visuals**

### Slide 10

**Text**

Wealth Management & Insurance – F'26 Strategic Priorities
Deeper Relationships
Deepen client relationships with CPB, CBB and TD Insurance, and drive referrals from Direct Invest to Advice businesses within Wealth
Expand advisor base in Wealth by leveraging talent development and acquisition programs
Accelerate Direct Investing client acquisition through the launch of the new TD Easy Trade app and enhancements to the Long-Term and Active Trader platforms
Drive mutual funds and ETF asset growth in Canada and drive Institutional asset management growth globally
Drive growth in profitable segments and regions in Insurance through digital marketing excellence and execution of new client acquisition strategies
Simpler & Faster
Simplify client experiences in Wealth by deploying new self-serve, digital onboarding and account funding capabilities
Increase advisor capacity in Wealth by reinventing businesses process and launching new digital capabilities, leveraging AI
Complete the unification of Private Wealth Management's discretionary offerings and realize $40MM of run-rate platform savings
Introduce new client-facing AI capabilities in Insurance to drive increased adoption of digital self-service tools and increase digital share of transactions
Disciplined Execution
Reduce structural costs in Wealth through productivity initiatives, distribution transformation and leveraging automation and AI
Strengthen risk and control environment by modernizing infrastructure and enhancing governance
Sustainably lower expense base in Insurance by re-envisioning business processes with AI and increasing automation
Mitigate losses from climate change in Insurance by managing geographic footprint and supporting clients with risk-reduction advice
9
— TD INVESTOR PRESENTATION Q1 2026

**Layout**

Three equal-width columns of pale green panels sit side by side below a single-line title at the top left, with the TD logo at the top right. Each column has a bold heading with an underline bar above its body text; the panels span roughly 4% to 96% of width and 15% to 92% of height, with the footer line at the bottom left under the panels.

none

### Slide 12

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Q1 results are presented as a grid of large headline figures, each with reported and adjusted values side by side, making the reconciliation easy to scan.
- Review: Slides 7 and 12 are well organised and legible, yet slide 12 mixes eight metrics, four updates and six targets with little emphasis on what matters most.
- What would change the score: I would split or trim the densest pages (slides 12, 36, 39, 41, 43) and move the long forward-looking text on slide 2 and the endnotes on slides 49 to 54 into a handout, with the first slide's empty lower area and the sparse slide 55 tidied.

This Slide contains forward-looking information. Please refer to the caution at the beginning of this presentation and the endnotes for this slide for material factors, risks and assumptions.

**Layout**

Four-column dashboard on a white page with the title top-left at about 3.5% from the left edge and 10% from the top. Two rows of four light-green metric boxes sit in a row band from about 7% to 92% across, followed by a centred 'Q1 2026 Updates' heading with a rule and four centred text columns, then a centred "F'26 Targets" heading with a rule and a six-cell green band above the footer. The TD logo sits at the top right corner and the page number and footer label sit at the bottom left, with the forward-looking note centred-left near the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Visuals: a small TD logo at the top right, a square green tile about 3.3% wide by 5.2% high, with a white TD monogram, placed at x 95.0 y 3.9 (image 42 by 37 pixels). Eight pale green rectangles (#EFF6E4) hold the metric figures: Net Income, EPS, PTPP Growth (YoY), Total Assets in the upper row; Efficiency Ratio, ROE, ROTCE, CET1 in the lower row. Two thin green horizontal rules (#007400, about 0.3% stroke) run under the Q1 2026 Updates and F'26 Targets headings, each slightly tilted, rising to the right. One pale green band (#EFF6E4) holds six target cells separated by thin grey-green vertical dividers (about 0.1% width, roughly #8FB08F) at about x 24.2, 37.6, 51.3, 65.7 and 79.2. No charts, photographs or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles and key figures are bold Arial (Helvetica-style sans-serif) in green: main title #008A00, section headings and metric labels #007400. Body and figure values are regular Arial in dark green #03522C. Main title is about 5.2% of slide height, section headings about 3.0%, metric labels about 2.6%, body text about 2.4%, metric values about 2.1%, target figures about 3.3% bold, footer about 1.5%. Metric boxes and target band are flat pale green #EFF6E4 with square corners and no shadow. Forward-looking note is italic Arial in grey #A6A6A6. Page number is #1C1C1C and footer label is black #000000.

### Slide 13

**Text**

Driving structural cost reduction across TD
Completed Restructuring Program
Program size
$200MM pre-tax / $148MM after-tax restructuring charges in Q1'26
Total restructuring program charges of $886MM pre-tax
Impact on expenses
Expect F'26 savings of ~$500MM pre-tax1
Expect fully realized annual cost savings of ~$775MM pre-tax1 (87% savings yield)
Creates capacity to invest to evolve the Bank and accelerate capabilities
Savings drivers
Employee severance and other personnel-related costs, real estate optimization, asset impairment and other rationalization, including certain business wind-downs
~3% FTE reduction including attrition and talent redeployment
Disciplined Cost Management
Targeting ~$2-2.5B in annualized cost savings over the medium-term, delivered across 6 key cost initiatives: (1) Distribution Transformation, (2) Automation & AI, (3) Technology & Data Modernization, (4) Procurement, (5) Global Delivery Workforce, & (6) Cost Moderation2
Expect F'26 savings of ~$900MM pre-tax (~$400MM savings in addition to ~$500MM restructuring savings)2
This Slide contains forward-looking information. Please refer to the caution at the beginning of this presentation and the endnotes for this slide for material factors, risks and assumptions.
12
— TD INVESTOR PRESENTATION Q1 2026

**Layout**

Title sits top-left at about 3.5% from the left edge, above a two-part body. Two full-width dark green bands (centred white headings) at about 21% and 70% of height frame three rows on the upper part: light green label boxes on the left (about 5% to 33% width) with bullet text in a right column starting about 37% across. Lower band is followed by left-aligned bullets. Footer at bottom left: page number 12 and a dash with the running line 'TD INVESTOR PRESENTATION Q1 2026'; grey italic disclaimer below it, starting about 6.7% across. TD logo sits in the top-right corner, about 3% from the top and right edges.

**Visuals**

One small illustration-free logo: the TD bank mark, a green square (#00A000 approx.) about 3.3% wide by 5.2% high in the top-right corner, containing white stylised TD letters. No charts, tables, photographs or diagrams. Five filled rectangles act as shapes: two dark green section bands, and three pale green label boxes behind the row labels. Bullets are small filled dark green squares (Wingdings square glyph).

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient. Title: bold Arial-style grotesque sans-serif, green #008A00, about 5.2% of slide height, left aligned. Section headers: bold sans-serif, white #FFFFFF on dark green #006700 bands, about 2.6% of height, centred. Row labels: bold sans-serif, dark green #03522C, about 3.3% of height. Body bullets: regular sans-serif (Arial), dark green #03522C, about 2.6% of height, line spacing about 1.2 times the size. Pale green label boxes #EFF6E4 with square corners and no shadow or border. Disclaimer in italic grey #A6A6A6 about 1.67%. Footer text in near-black about 1.5%. Superscript footnote markers are small numerals about 1.7% high.

13
— TD INVESTOR PRESENTATION Q1 2026

**Layout**

Single-column title band with the title at top left and a TD logo in the top right corner. A full-width green chevron banner sits under the title, above a horizontal timeline with five year pins, followed by two columns of bullets (left: Examples of Completed Actions; right: Examples of what is in flight) with a pale green full-width band for the closing statement near the bottom. Left margin about 3.5%, footer line at about 92.7% height with page number 13 at left and the italic note in the lower right of the content area; a disclaimer line sits at the very bottom.

**Visuals**

Five green map-pin icons placed along a horizontal dark green timeline bar, one above each year label, at the x positions of the years 2024, 2025, 2026, 2027 and 2028. The timeline bar fades from dark green #006700 at left to white at its right end. Above the timeline, a green chevron-tipped banner (#006700) spans most of the width. Below the timeline, a grey (#A6A6A6) right-pointing chevron box spans from the 2025 pin area to the right edge and contains white text. Two green headings sit over horizontal dark green rules, one per column. Bullets are small green square markers. A small TD logo (bright green square with white TD letters) sits at top right. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles and headings in bold Arial (sans-serif) green #008A00; main title about 5.2% of slide height, section headings about 3.2%, body text about 2.3%, closing statement bold about 2.8%. Body text dark green #03522C with bold emphasis words in the same colour. Accent colours: dark green #006700 banner, rules and timeline; grey #A6A6A6 chevron and disclaimer; pale green #EFF6E4 closing band. Year labels bold black Arial about 2.6%. Shapes are flat with no shadows except slight shadow under the pins; chevron tips are sharp, no rounded corners.

Executing AI-at-scale and innovating for our Clients
AI
TD Invent
Aim to deliver $1B in value¹
TD Invent
Our strategic, bank-wide umbrella effort to power innovation
Medium-term target value¹
~$500MM
&

Annualized
Revenue Uplift
Annualized
Cost Savings
• U.S. Banking now offers Shop with Points at Amazon in the U.S. for eligible TD credit cardholders to instantly redeem their points at checkout on Amazon.com.
• KONEK, developed with Canada's leading FIs, including TD, and powered by Interac, provides a secure, flexible multi-rail online payment solution. It is now live with its first national merchant and several small businesses.²
• TD is the first FI to join leading companies as a funding member of OIN 2.0., expanding OIN's patent cross-licensing model beyond Linux OSS packages relating to AI, cyber, cloud and other new technologies.³
• TD's patent portfolio now includes 3,600 assets, reflecting a 20-year commitment to disciplined, long-term innovation.
• 2026 BIG Innovation Awards: Recognized across multiple categories, including Organizational Innovation for Colleague Onboarding and Individual Innovation honours for AI leadership⁴

15 — TD INVESTOR PRESENTATION Q1 2026

**Layout**

Three equal columns of framed panels sit side by side below a single left-aligned green title that spans the top of the slide, with the TD logo in the top-right corner. Each column has a full-width dark green header bar with a centred white label, and the panels run from the header bottom down to about 91% of the slide height. Each panel holds two stacked chart groups with centred green headings, and the footer carries the slide number and a grey forward-looking disclaimer at the bottom left.

**Visuals**

Six small bar charts, all with the same three-bar structure: a Q1/25 bar (light grey), a Q1/26 bar (light green), and a Medium-term target bar (mid green) with its value printed inside in white. Each chart has a horizontal baseline and no y-axis, gridlines or legend. Each chart carries a black-outlined oval callout with an arrow connecting the Q1/25 bar top to the Q1/26 bar top showing the change. Canada Mobile Users (millions): Q1/25 8.2, Q1/26 8.7, target 11, callout +5.0%. Canada Self-Serve Transactions (% of all financial transactions): Q1/25 93.0, Q1/26 93.8, target 96, callout +80bps. U.S. Mobile Users (millions): Q1/25 5.1, Q1/26 5.2, target 6+, callout +1.8%. U.S. Self-Serve Transactions (% of all financial transactions): Q1/25 84.2, Q1/26 86.0, target 90, callout +180bps. Home & Auto Clients Digitally Engaged (% of total customers): Q1/25 71.9, Q1/26 79.2, target 90+, callout +730 bps. TD MyInsurance Transactions (millions): LTM Q1/25 1.4, LTM Q1/26 1.9, target 3+, callout +39%. Superscript footnote numbers appear in the headings and labels. No logos other than the TD logo at top right, which is a green square with a white TD monogram.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with dark green headers and framed panels. Titles and headings are bold Arial-style sans-serif in green #008A00; the panel headers use white bold text on #006700. Value labels and axis labels are bold sans-serif in dark green #03522C; callout text and disclaimer are regular sans-serif in #1C1C1C and #A6A6A6. Bars: Q1/25 #F2F2F2, Q1/26 #A8DBA0, medium-term target #58AA48. Title is about 5.2% of slide height, headings about 2.6%, value labels about 1.7%, footer about 1.5%. Panels have thin #D9D9D9 borders with square corners and no shadow. Callout ovals have a 0.75 pt black outline with white fill.

### Slide 17

**Text**

TD logo / More human. / Q1 2026 Brand and Sustainability Highlights / CHARM INDUSTRIAL / Announced multi-year agreement with Charm to purchase carbon removal credits / MassDevelopment / Purchased ~US$23MM MassDevelopment bond to support affordable housing in Massachusetts / Announced expansion of low-cost and no-cost banking options for Indigenous Peoples from Canada / 2026 / Brand Finance® / TOP 100 GLOBAL BRAND / 1 / 16 — TD INVESTOR PRESENTATION Q1 2026

**Layout**

Title sits top-left at about 10% from the top with its left edge at 3.2%, left-aligned, on a single line. The TD tile is top-right at 3.9% from the top. The left column holds the TD More human. lockup at 26.7% and the Brand Finance badge below it, starting at 46.3%. The right side is a two-column arrangement of partner logos (Charm, MassDevelopment) with centred captions, and a single centred icon block with caption below at about 60% to 85%. Footer sits bottom-left at about 92.7% with the page number 16, an em dash and the footer text. No slide number other than the printed 16.

**Visuals**

Five logo and image elements and one icon. (1) TD 'More human.' lockup: a solid bright green (about #33BB00) square with the white stylised 'TD' letters, followed by the black bold sans-serif wordmark 'More human.' with a period; the lockup is about 41% of slide width, placed at x 5.9 y 26.7. (2) CHARM INDUSTRIAL logo: a dark grey (about #4D4D4D) four-leaf clover/ clover-knot emblem to the left of the word 'CHARM' in a grey (about #4D4D4D) bold serif with 'INDUSTRIAL' in small caps below between double rules; at x 50.8 y 29.9 w 20.2 h 8.5. (3) MassDevelopment logo: a stacked geometric emblem of three interlocking blocks in navy (about #0B2E5E), cyan blue (about #1B9AD6) and lime green (about #7DC242) to the left of the wordmark 'MassDevelopment' in navy (about #0B2E5E) on the 'Mass' part and cyan (about #1B9AD6) on 'Development'; at x 72.5 y 30.3 w 23.3 h 6.7. (4) Brand Finance Top 100 Global Brand badge: a navy (about #1F3F8F) laurel wreath around a shield with a wave pattern and three dots, '2026' above the shield in navy, 'Brand Finance®' in navy below, then 'TOP 100' and 'GLOBAL BRAND' in navy capitals; the badge is shown on a white panel with a thin light-grey (about #D9D9D9) left edge and top edge line, placed at x 16.5 y 46.3 w 18.6 h 39.0 (265 by 312 px), not cropped, unboxed except for that grey edge, with the superscript '1' to its right at about x 32.7 y 78.4. (5) Key-and-hand icon: a bright green (about #1DB954) key with a dollar sign in the bow, above a dark green (about #0B6B3A) outlined open hand; the icon sits on a very pale green (about #EEF7EC) square tile at x 67.5 y 60.5 w 10.1 h 12.7 (102 by 72 px). Lighting is flat, no shadows; no photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background, no gradients, shadows or textures. Title in bold Arial-style sans-serif (Helvetica/Arial Bold) in green #008A00, about 5.2% of slide height, cap height about 3.6% of height. Captions in bold Arial, dark green #03522C, about 2.2% of slide height, centred. Footer text in regular Arial, #1C1C1C for page number and #000000 for footer text, about 1.5% of slide height. Corner logo tile in bright green #33BB00 with white TD letters, square, no rounding. Shapes have no outlines apart from the thin light-grey edge on the Brand Finance panel. Brand accent colour green throughout.

### Slide 20

**Text**

History of strong credit quality / Net impaired loans and PCL ratios (bps) / Net impaired loans as a % of net loans and acceptances (bps) / Provision for credit losses as a % of net average loans and acceptances (bps) / 100 / 48 / 46 / 46 / 45 / 47 / Q1'26 / 41 43 / 38 / 39 / 32 / 34 / 41 / 40 Q1'26 / 37 / 37 / 34 / 34 / 33 / 36 / 41 / 20 / 25 / 24 / 14 / -3 / IFRS 9 / 2014 / 2015 / 2016 / 2017 / 2018 / 2019 / 2020 / 2021 / 2022 / 2023 / 2024 / 2025 / 19 / — TD INVESTOR PRESENTATION Q1 2026 / 1

**Layout**

Single chart slide on a white background. The title sits top-left at about 3.7% from the left edge, aligned left, with the TD logo boxed in the top-right corner. A full-width dark green header bar spans x 3.1% to 96.8% with a centred bar label, and below it a line chart fills the width with its x-axis year labels near the bottom. The footer with page number 19 and the document name sits bottom-left at about 4% from the left edge.

**Visuals**

Line chart titled 'Net impaired loans and PCL ratios (bps)' with two series plotted over years 2014 to 2025 and a final Q1'26 point. Series 1, 'Net impaired loans as a % of net loans and acceptances (bps)', is a bright green line with circle markers and labels: 2014 46, 2015 48, 2016 46, 2017 38, 2018 39, 2019 33, 2020 32, 2021 24, 2022 20, 2023 25, 2024 36, 2025 40, Q1'26 41. Series 2, 'Provision for credit losses as a % of net average loans and acceptances (bps)', is a dark green line with circle markers and labels: 2014 34, 2015 34, 2016 41, 2017 37, 2018 37, 2019 45, 2020 100, 2021 -3, 2022 14, 2023 34, 2024 47, 2025 41, Q1'26 43. No y-axis, gridlines or scale are printed; values are shown only as labels. The x-axis is a thin light grey baseline with small tick marks at each year. A vertical dashed dark green line at about x 2019 start separates pre-IFRS 9 from IFRS 9 years, and the area to its right is shaded pale green #E6F1E0 with the label 'IFRS 9' (superscript 1) in grey #7F7F7F. The highest point 2020 (100) and the low 2021 (-3) are the extremes; no points are otherwise highlighted. Legend is top-left: a bright green line with dot marker for series 1 in bold, and a dark green line with dot marker for series 2 in regular weight. The TD logo is a solid green square #00B200-like tile with white 'TD' letters, about 3.3% wide.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF with a pale green shaded panel #E6F1E0 behind the IFRS 9 period. Title text is bold sans-serif (Arial Bold) in green #008A00, large, about 5.2% of slide height. Header bar is solid dark green #03522C with white bold Arial text about 2.6% of slide height. Chart labels and axis years are dark green #03522C in Arial Bold (values) and Arial regular (years), about 2.0% and 1.7% of slide height. Series 1 line is bright green #008A00-ish; series 2 is darker green #03522C-ish. Lines are about 2 pt weight with round markers. **Layout**

A single full-width table is placed below a left-aligned title, with the title starting at about 3.5% from the left edge and the table spanning from about 4% to 95.4% of slide width. The TD logo sits in the top-right corner, and the footer (page number 20 and the TD investor presentation line) sits bottom-left at about 92.7% of slide height.

**Visuals**

A single table with three columns and 25 body rows plus one header row. Header row: 'Period-End Balances ($B unless otherwise noted)' spanning the label column, 'Q4/25' and 'Q1/26' as right-aligned value columns. Rows, label then Q4/25 then Q1/26: Canadian Personal and Commercial Portfolio 614.5 619.3 (bold); Personal 479.5 482.1 (bold, indented); Residential Mortgages 264.5 258.8; Home Equity Lines of Credit (HELOC) 147.9 156.4; Indirect Auto 32.1 32.0; Credit Cards 21.9 21.9; Other Personal 13.1 13.0; Unsecured Lines of Credit 10.6 10.5 (italic, further indented); Commercial Banking (including Small Business Banking) 135.0 137.2 (bold); U.S. Banking Portfolio (all amounts in US$) 175.0 172.9 (bold); Personal 89.4 89.7 (bold); Residential Mortgages 34.0 34.0; Home Equity Lines of Credit (HELOC) with superscript 1, 8.9 9.0; Indirect Auto 31.5 31.7; Credit Cards 14.1 14.1; Other Personal 0.9 0.9; Commercial Banking 85.6 83.2 (bold); Non-residential Real Estate 18.4 18.0; Residential Real Estate 10.2 10.2; Commercial & Industrial (C&I) 57.0 55.0; FX on U.S. Personal & Commercial Portfolio 70.2 62.1 (bold); U.S. Banking Portfolio ($) 245.2 235.0 (bold); Canadian Wealth Management and Insurance Portfolio 9.0 9.8 (bold); Wholesale Portfolio 93.3 103.4 (bold); Total with superscript 2, 962.0 967.5 (bold). **Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate investor slide. Title is bold green in a sans-serif (Arial Bold style), about 5.2% of slide height. Table header bar is solid dark green #006700 with white bold Arial text about 2.6% of slide height. Table body text is dark green #03522C in Arial regular, about 2.0% of slide height, with bold for subtotal rows and values about 1.8%. Thin green horizontal rules separate rows. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners. Footer text is Arial regular about 1.5% of slide height, page number in #1C1C1C and footer line in black.

**Text**

Allowance for Credit Losses (ACL)
Highlights
ACL decreased $144 million quarter-over-quarter, reflecting:
– A $156 million impact of foreign exchange
– Improvement in the macroeconomic forecasts
– Partially offset by higher impaired allowance in the Wholesale Banking and U.S. commercial lending portfolios
While there are many potential scenarios that may impact the economic trajectory and credit performance, the Bank's fiscal 2026 PCLs are expected to be in the range of 40 to 50 basis points, consistent with prior guidance5

21 — TD INVESTOR PRESENTATION Q1 2026

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: a narrow left text column (Highlights and bullets, roughly x 3 to 33%) separated by a thin vertical grey rule at about x 35% from a right column (x 42 to 92%) holding a stacked ACL chart on top and an ACL by asset type column chart with a data table beneath. The title sits top-left at x 3.7%, y 6.9%, left-aligned, with the TD logo top-right at x 92.6%, y 3.9%. The footer is a page number and document label at bottom-left (y about 92.7%) and a grey italic forward-looking disclaimer across the bottom (y about 96.6%); margins are about 3 to 4% left and 5% right.

**Visuals**

Chart 1, titled 'ACL¹: $B and Coverage Ratios²' (top right): a stacked column chart of five quarterly columns, Q1/25, Q2/25, Q3/25, Q4/25 and Q1/26, with no printed value axis or gridlines; the baseline is a thin dark line. Each column's total is printed above it: $9.6, $9.6, $9.7, $9.7, $9.6. Each column has two segments: the bottom Canada segment in dark green #008A00 with labels 43%, 44%, 45%, 45%, 46% (white text), and the top United States segment in light green #AED99E with labels 57%, 56%, 55%, 55%, 54% (dark green text). Above the columns is a dark green #008A00 line with circular markers and labels 99, 101, 103, 101, 99 bps (the last reads '99 bps'); the line has no printed axis. Legend at upper left: light green square labelled United States, dark green square labelled Canada. Chart 2, titled 'ACL by Asset Type: $B' (lower right): a clustered column chart with five categories, Residential Mortgages, Consumer instalment and other personal⁴, Credit Card, Business & Government and Total. Series Q4/25 is light green #AED99E and Q1/26 is dark green #008A00; a grey (#A6A6A6-like) segment marked $1.0 sits at the base of the Credit Card and Total columns for U.S. Strategic Card Partners' Share³. Labelled values: Residential Mortgages $0.4 and $0.4; Consumer $2.3 and $2.2; Credit Card $2.8 and $2.8; Business & Government $4.3 and $4.2; Total $9.7 and $9.6. No value axis is printed; column heights are proportional to these values. Legend at upper left lists Q4/25 (light green), Q1/26 (dark green) and U.S. Strategic Card Partners' Share³ (grey). Table beneath the chart: 4 rows with no header row printed; columns are row label then values in pairs. Row 1 'Performing ($B)': 0.28, 0.30, 2.0, 1.9, 2.3, 2.3, 3.5, 3.3, 8.1, 7.9. Row 2 'Impaired ($B)': 0.08, 0.09, 0.3, 0.3, 0.5, 0.5, 0.8, 0.9, 1.6, 1.7. Row 3 'Ratio³ (bps)': 11, 12, 88, 83, 670, 681, 125, 120, 101, 99. The table has thin light grey cell dividers and a dark green #006700 outer border. Logo: TD wordmark in a bright green square at top right, white 'TD' letters, roughly 42 by 37 pixels.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background with a faint pale green #E5F1D4 layer that is fully covered by white. Title in bold green #008A00, sans-serif (Arial Bold), about 5.2% of slide height, with headings 'Highlights' and chart titles in dark green #03522C Arial Bold at about 2.6%. Body text is Arial regular in dark green #03522C at about 2.2%; chart labels about 1.6 to 1.8%; table text about 1.84%. Accent colours: #008A00 bright green, #006700 deep green for table frame, #AED99E light green for pale series, #A6A6A6 grey for the disclaimer and the Strategic Card Partners segment. Bullets are small solid squares (Wingdings ▪) in dark green; sub-bullets are en dashes in bright green #008A00. Shapes are flat with no corner radii, shadows or gradients; the divider rule is a thin solid grey line.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: a narrow left text column (Highlights) occupies roughly the left 35% and a stacked-column chart occupies the right 58%, separated by a thin vertical grey rule at about x 35%, running from y 20% to y 93%. The title block sits top-left at x 3.7%, with the title at y 6.9% and subtitle below at y 13.3%, left-aligned, with the TD logo boxed at top right (x 92.6%, y 3.9%). Left margin is about 3.7%; the footer (page number 22 and document label) sits bottom-left at y 92.7%. No slide number is printed in the footer other than the page number 22.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked column chart with an overlaid line series, titled 'GIL: $MM and Ratios' with superscript footnote marker 1. The category axis shows Q1/25, Q2/25, Q3/25, Q4/25, Q1/26 with a horizontal baseline in dark green; no numeric y-axis is printed and no axis range is shown. Stacks from bottom to top: U.S. Banking (light green #AED99E), Canadian Personal & Commercial Banking (green #008A00), Wholesale Banking (dark green #006700), Wealth Management & Insurance (light grey #BFBFBF). Printed values, US Banking: Q1/25 $3,028 / 109 bps; Q2/25 $2,714 / 109 bps; Q3/25 $2,865 / 116 bps; Q4/25 $2,903 / 118 bps; Q1/26 $3,011 / 128 bps. Canadian Personal & Commercial Banking: Q1/25 $1,999 / 34 bps; Q2/25 $1,820 / 31 bps; Q3/25 $1,865 / 31 bps; Q4/25 $1,942 / 32 bps; Q1/26 $1,994 / 32 bps. Wholesale Banking (callouts with leader lines): Q1/25 $421 / 41 bps; Q2/25 $327 / 34 bps; Q3/25 $600 / 69 bps; Q4/25 $572 / 61 bps; Q1/26 $586 / 57 bps. Wealth Management & Insurance (thin grey band): Q1/25 $5 / 6 bps; Q2/25 $5 / 7 bps; Q3/25 $4 / 5 bps; Q4/25 $3 / 3 bps; Q1/26 $3 / 3 bps. Column totals in bold above each stack: $5,453; $4,866; $5,334; $5,420; $5,594. Line series in dark green (#008A00) with circular markers, values above each point: 56, 51, 56, 56, 58 bps (only the last point carries the unit 'bps'). Legend below the chart in two rows with small square swatches: U.S. Banking; Canadian Personal & Commercial Banking; Wholesale Banking; Wealth Management & Insurance. No points are highlighted. No photographs or icons other than the TD logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Titles in TD green #008A00 (bold sans-serif, Arial Bold style). Section heading 'Highlights' and chart title in dark green #03522C, bold sans-serif. Body text in dark green #03522C, regular sans-serif (Arial style). Bullets are small green squares (Wingdings) in #008A00 for level one and en dash in #008A00 for level two. Chart labels in dark green #03522C with white labels inside the two darker bars. Accent colours: #AED99E light green, #008A00 green, #006700 dark green, #BFBFBF grey. Shapes are plain with square corners, no shadows, no gradients. Title approx 5.2% of slide height; body text approx 2.2% of slide height; chart labels approx 1.8% of slide height.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: a narrow left text column (Highlights bullets) at about x 3.7 to 27 percent, a thin vertical divider at x 35 percent, and a right column at x 40 to 94 percent holding the stacked column chart above a full-width PCL ratio table. Title and subtitle are left-aligned at x 3.7 percent, stacked at the top with the title above the subtitle; the TD logo sits in the top-right corner at x 92.6 percent. Footer with page number 23 and the TD investor presentation line sits bottom-left at y about 92.7 percent; margins about 3.7 percent left and 4 percent top.

**Visuals**

Stacked column chart titled PCL: $MM and Ratios with superscript 1,2,3, five columns for Q1/25, Q2/25, Q3/25, Q4/25 and Q1/26, each with a bold total above it ($1,212, $1,341, $971, $982, $1,039). Segments from bottom to top are Corporate, U.S. Banking (net), Canadian Personal & Commercial Banking, Wholesale Banking and a top segment; the legend lists Wealth Management & Insurance, Wholesale Banking, Canadian Personal & Commercial Banking, U.S. Banking (net) and Corporate, top to bottom. Printed values by column: Q1/25 Corporate $168, U.S. Banking (net) $451, Canadian Personal & Commercial Banking $521, Wholesale Banking $72; Q2/25 $154, $442, $622, $123; Q3/25 $120, $317, $463, $71; Q4/25 $117, $304, $537, $24; Q1/26 $136, $295, $436, $172. Wealth Management & Insurance has no visible segment. No y-axis or axis labels are printed; the x-axis is a baseline with tick marks at column boundaries. Table titled PCL Ratio (bps) with columns Q1/25, Q2/25, Q3/25, Q4/25, Q1/26 and rows: Canadian Personal & Commercial Banking 35, 44, 31, 35, 28; U.S. Banking (net)2 67, 70, 52, 50, 49; U.S. Banking & Corporate (gross)3 92, 94, 72, 70, 72; Wholesale Banking 29, 51, 31, 11, 70; Total Bank (gross)3 bold 50, 58, 41, 41, 43; Total Bank (net)2,4 bold white on dark green 43, 51, 36, 36, 38. The TD logo is a green square tile with white TD letters in the top right. A thin grey vertical rule divides the slide. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF (a pale green #E5F1D4 rectangle lies beneath it and is fully covered). White background. Titles and accent in TD green #008A00, body and table text in dark green #03522C, bars in greens #008A00, #006700, #AED99E and grey-green #739283. Typeface is Arial (Helvetica-like sans-serif), bold for title, subtitle, headings and totals, regular for body. Title about 5.2 percent of slide height, subtitle about 3.7 percent, section headings about 3.0 percent, body and chart labels about 1.8 to 2.2 percent. Table header and rules are dark green, with a solid dark green total row. Square bullets in green. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

**Layout**

Title sits top-left at about 3% margin, with the TD logo in the top-right corner. Below it, a 3-column by 2-row grid of six line-chart panels is framed by a thin light-green border, with a green header row across the top and grey section bands between the two rows. Columns are Canada, U.S. and Total Bank; the rows are Personal and Business & Government (B&G). Each panel has a horizontal baseline with Q1/25 to Q1/26 labels beneath and a legend below that. The footer with page number 25 sits bottom-left, and there is no visible slide number in the top corner.

**Visuals**

Six small line charts, one per grid panel, with no printed y-axis, no gridlines and no axis ranges. Each chart has five points at Q1/25, Q2/25, Q3/25, Q4/25 and Q1/26 joined by a solid dark-green line (#008A00) with filled circular markers, and each point carries its printed value above it. The Q1/26 value is bold in every panel. Panel 1, Canadian Personal (legend 'Canadian Personal'): 0.30%, 0.29%, 0.26%, 0.30%, 0.31%. Panel 2, U.S. Personal: 1.60%, 1.21%, 1.22%, 1.31%, 1.31%; the Q2'25 label is printed as Q2'25. Panel 3, Total Personal: 0.59%, 0.49%, 0.45%, 0.50%, 0.51%. Panel 4, Canadian B&G: 0.23%, 0.23%, 0.12%, 0.18%, 0.10%. Panel 5, U.S. B&G: 0.49%, 0.27%, 0.44%, 0.26%, 0.87%. Panel 6, Total B&G: 0.35%, 0.27%, 0.26%, 0.21%, 0.45%. Each legend shows a short line with a circular marker in the series colour, followed by the series name. Nothing is highlighted apart from the bold Q1/26 values. The TD logo is a green square (#00A400) with white stylised 'TD' letters in the top-right corner.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with dark-green (#006700) header bars and a green title. Titles and accents are green #008A00; the header text is white; the data labels are dark green #004D00 and the category labels are #03522C. Body and legend text is near-black #1C1C1C. The typeface is Arial Bold for the title, subtitle, column headers and category labels, and Arial Regular for data labels, section labels and legends. The title is about 5.2% of slide height, subtitle about 3.7%, section and column headers about 2%, and data labels and legends about 1.8%. Shapes are flat rectangles with no rounded corners, shadows or gradients. Section bands are #F2F2F2. Dividers are thin mid-grey vertical rules. The outer frame is a thin light-green line (#A8D8A8 approx.). The chart lines are about 0.3% of slide height thick.

### Slide 27

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A regional table of loan-to-value by mortgage and HELOC type, shown for Q4/25 and Q1/26 in adjacent columns, lets the reader compare periods within each region.
- Review: Slides 27, 29, 30 and 31 give one thin highlight bullet beside large tables, so the audience must find the point themselves, and many pages (25, 26, 34, 39, 41, 43) are very small text or dense blocks that are hard to read live.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent green header and footer system, the clean banded tables on slides 27 and 30, and the three-pillar structure with targets strip on slides 7 and 8.
- Suggested repair: Rewrite topic titles as takeaway statements and give each table or chart slide a stated conclusion, especially slides 27 to 31 where one short bullet sits beside a large table.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: a left column (about x 3 to 40) holds the title block and the Highlights bullet, and a right column (about x 43 to 93) holds two stacked tables. The slide title sits top-left at the top margin, aligned with the left edge of the Highlights heading. A thin vertical grey divider runs at about x 41 from y 18 to y 93, separating the columns. The TD logo sits in the top-right corner, and the footer with page number 26 sits bottom-left at about y 93.

**Visuals**

Two tables, no charts, photographs or diagrams. Table 1 is titled Canadian Personal Banking (Q1/26) with superscript 1. Columns: row label, Gross Loans ($B), GIL ($MM), GIL/Loans (%). Rows: Residential Mortgages 258.8 / 467 / 0.18; Home Equity Lines of Credit (HELOC) 156.4 / 234 / 0.15; Indirect Auto 32.0 / 128 / 0.40; Credit Cards 21.9 / 179 / 0.82; Other Personal 13.0 / 74 / 0.57; Unsecured Lines of Credit (italic, indented) 10.5 / 48 / 0.46; Total Canadian Personal Banking (bold) 482.1 / 1,082 / 0.22; Change vs. Q4/25 (indented) 2.6 / 97 / 0.01. Header row is filled #006700 with white bold text. Body rows are white with thin green #006700 horizontal rules. Table 2 is titled Canadian RESL Portfolio – Loan to Value by Region (%) with superscript 2, 3. It has a two-tier header: top row Q4/25 spanning Mortgage, HELOC, Total RESL and Q1/26 spanning Mortgage, HELOC, Total RESL, both on #006700 fill with white bold text; a row-label column with no header text. Rows: Atlantic 61 52 57 | 63 54 59; BC 59 50 55 | 61 52 56; Ontario 61 50 55 | 63 52 57; Prairies 61 51 57 | 62 53 58; Quebec 60 56 58 | 61 57 59; Canada (bold) 61 51 56 | 62 53 57. No chart, no highlighted points, no legend. The logo is a small square green icon with white bold TD letters. A thin white gap separates the Q4/25 and Q1/26 column groups.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide. Title in Arial Bold, green #008A00, about 5.2% of slide height. Section headings Highlights and the two table titles in Arial Bold, dark green #006600, about 3.3% of slide height. Bullet text in Arial regular, dark green #03522C, about 2.2% of slide height. Table text in Arial regular, green #006700 for row labels and #03522C or #004500 for numbers, about 1.7% of slide height. Table headers are white bold Arial, about 1.8%. Bullet marker is a green Wingdings square #008A00. Footer text is Arial regular, about 1.5%, with page number #1C1C1C and footer text black #000000. Lines are thin green rules and a thin grey vertical divider. No corner radii, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 28

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Rewrite topic titles as takeaway statements and give each table or chart slide a stated conclusion, especially slides 27 to 31 where one short bullet sits beside a large table.

**Text**

Canadian Real Estate Secured Lending Portfolio
Highlights
▪ Total Canadian real estate secured lending portfolio at $418B
– 91% of RESL portfolio is amortizing4
− 76% of HELOC portfolio is amortizing
– 42% variable interest rate, of which 21% Mortgage and 21% HELOC
– 14% of RESL portfolio insured
▪ Canadian RESL credit quality remained strong
– Five-year average impaired loss rate ~1bp
– Uninsured average Bureau score5 of 792, largely stable quarter-over-quarter
– Less than 1% of the RESL portfolio is uninsured, has a bureau score of 650 or lower and LTV greater than 75%
▪ Condo and Investor6 RESL credit quality consistent with broader portfolio
– Condo RESL represented ~15% of RESL outstanding with 18% insured
– Investor RESL represented ~12% of RESL outstanding

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white page with a title across the top-left, a thin vertical grey divider at about 41% of the slide width separating a left Highlights column (x 2.8 to 39 percent) from a right column of three stacked sections (Maturity Schedule chart, Current Loan to Value table, Regional Breakdown chart) starting at about x 43.7. Title left-aligned at x 2.8 with its top at y 6.9; the TD logo sits top-right at x 92.6 and y 3.9. Footer line with slide number 27 and the source text sits bottom-left at y 92.7. Margins are about 3 percent on the left and 5 percent on the right.

**Visuals**

1) Stacked column chart titled Maturity Schedule ($B)1, five columns with total labels above: FY26 (Q2-Q4) total $78.9, variable $22.9, fixed $56.0, share of total amortizing balances 21.0%; FY27 total $102.9, variable $40.1, fixed $62.8, 27.4%; FY28 total $87.3, variable $19.7, fixed $67.6, 23.2%; FY29 total $38.2, variable $13.8, fixed $24.4, 10.2%; FY30+ total $68.3, variable $41.4, fixed $26.9, 18.2%. Fixed Rate segments dark green #008A00 at bottom, Variable Rate segments light green #AED99E on top. Legend at top-left of chart: Variable Rate (light green square), Fixed Rate (dark green square). No visible axes or gridlines; baseline is a thin grey line. Column bars roughly equal width.
2) Table titled Canadian RESL Portfolio – Current Loan to Value (%)2. Header row dark green #006600 fill with white bold labels Q1/25, Q2/25, Q3/25, Q4/25, Q1/26 across columns; blank top-left header cell. Row Uninsured: 53, 54, 54, 56, 57. Row Insured: 52, 53, 54, 55, 57. Thin green rule under the Uninsured row; no vertical rules.
3) Stacked column chart titled Regional Breakdown3 ($B), six visible bars left to right with labels: Atlantic $10 (76% uninsured light green, 24% insured dark green, share 3%); BC $85 (90% uninsured, 10% insured, share 20%); Ontario $231 (90% uninsured, 10% insured, share 55%); Prairies $56 (69% uninsured, 31% insured, share 14%); Quebec $36 (83% uninsured, 17% insured, share 8%). Legend top-left: Uninsured light green, Insured dark green. A curly brace on the right spans the bars labelled $418B. Leader lines connect the 24% and 10% and 17% labels to the small bar segments. Category labels Atlantic, BC, Ontario, Prairies, Quebec below the baseline; a row of percentages beneath with the caption % of RESL Portfolio at right. No axes or gridlines.
4) TD logo: white T and D letterforms on a green square, top-right.

### Slide 29

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 27, 29, 30 and 31 give one thin highlight bullet beside large tables, so the audience must find the point themselves, and many pages (25, 26, 34, 39, 41, 43) are very small text or dense blocks that are hard to read live.
- Suggested repair: Rewrite topic titles as takeaway statements and give each table or chart slide a stated conclusion, especially slides 27 to 31 where one short bullet sits beside a large table.

**Text**

– The Canadian Commercial Banking portfolio
– Partially offset by an increase in Wholesale Banking

**Layout**

Two columns separated by a thin vertical grey rule at about 41% of the slide width, running from about 19.6% to 93% of the height. The title sits top-left at about 6.9% from the top, with a TD logo box in the top-right corner. The left column holds the Highlights heading and bullets; the right column stacks two tables, the top table headed Canadian Commercial and Wholesale Banking (Q1/26) and the lower table headed Industry Breakdown with superscript 1. Left-aligned text with margins of about 2.8% on the left; the footer with printed page number 28 and the text TD INVESTOR PRESENTATION Q1 2026 sits bottom-left at about 92.7% from the top.

**Visuals**

Two tables, no charts. Table 1 (top right): columns are the row label column (blank header), Gross Loans ($B), GIL ($MM), GIL/Loans (%); header row filled dark green #006700 with white bold text. Rows: Commercial Banking1 137.2 / 912 / 0.66; Wholesale Banking 103.4 / 586 / 0.57; Total Canadian Commercial and Wholesale Banking (bold) 240.6 / 1,498 / 0.62; Change vs. Q4/25 12.3 / (31) / (0.05). Rows separated by thin green horizontal rules, white cell fill. Table 2 (lower right): columns are the row label column (blank header), Gross Loans ($B), GIL ($MM); header row filled #006700 with white bold text. Rows: Real Estate – Residential 29.5 / 5; Real Estate – Non-residential 30.2 / 57; Financial 53.6 / 5; Govt-PSE-Health & Social Services 18.6 / 77; Oil and Gas 3.4 / 8; Metals and Mining 4.2 / 49; Forestry 1.0 / 66; Consumer2 9.9 / 433; Industrial/Manufacturing3 13.6 / 264; Agriculture 13.8 / 25; Automotive 13.6 / 103; Other4 49.2 / 406; Total (bold) $240.6 / $1,498. Superscript numbers 1 to 4 are footnote markers with no footnote text printed on the slide. The TD logo at top right is a bright green square with white stylised TD letters. A thin grey vertical line separates the columns.

### Slide 30

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 27, 29, 30 and 31 give one thin highlight bullet beside large tables, so the audience must find the point themselves, and many pages (25, 26, 34, 39, 41, 43) are very small text or dense blocks that are hard to read live.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent green header and footer system, the clean banded tables on slides 27 and 30, and the three-pillar structure with targets strip on slides 7 and 8.
- Suggested repair: Rewrite topic titles as takeaway statements and give each table or chart slide a stated conclusion, especially slides 27 to 31 where one short bullet sits beside a large table.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: a left narrow column (about x 3% to 37%) holding the title block and a Highlights section with one bullet, and a right column (about x 44% to 89%) holding two stacked tables. The slide title sits top-left at y about 7% and is left-aligned at x 2.8%. A thin vertical grey divider runs at x about 41% from y about 20% to 93%. A TD logo sits top-right at x 92.6%, y 3.9%. The footer (page number 29 and the document line) sits bottom-left at y about 92.7%, with left margin about 3.9%.

Two data tables, no charts, photographs or diagrams. Table 1, titled 'U.S. Personal Banking (Q1/26)': columns are row label, Gross Loans ($B), GIL ($MM), GIL/Loans (%). **Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Title colour is green #008A00 in bold Arial-style sans-serif (Helvetica-like), about 5.2% of slide height. Section headings (Highlights, the two table titles) are dark green #006600 bold sans, about 3.3% to 3.7% of slide height. Body and table text is dark green #006700 regular sans, about 1.7% to 2.2% of slide height. Table headers are dark green #006700 fill with white bold sans text. Table rules are thin light-green horizontal lines between rows, with no vertical rules. The vertical divider is a thin grey line about #9A9A9A. Footer text is near-black #1C1C1C and #000000, about 1.5% of slide height. No shadows, rounded corners or gradients.

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 27, 29, 30 and 31 give one thin highlight bullet beside large tables, so the audience must find the point themselves, and many pages (25, 26, 34, 39, 41, 43) are very small text or dense blocks that are hard to read live.
- Suggested repair: Rewrite topic titles as takeaway statements and give each table or chart slide a stated conclusion, especially slides 27 to 31 where one short bullet sits beside a large table.

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a white page: a narrow left column holds the Highlights heading and a single bullet, separated by a thin vertical grey rule at about x 41 from a right area holding one wide table at top and two side-by-side tables below. ### Slide 32

**Text**

TD Bank Group / Key Themes / Overview of TD Bank Group / Credit Update / Strong Balance Sheet & Capital Position / 31 — TD INVESTOR PRESENTATION Q1 2026

**Layout**

Two-zone layout: a full-height photograph fills the left 60.1% of the slide and a white field fills the right. The title block sits top-left of the right zone at about 10% from the top, with the TD logo at the top-right corner. Three equal light-green menu bars stack vertically from 33% to 85% of the height, starting at 42.7% from the left and running to the right edge with no margin. The footer sits bottom-left over the photograph at about 93% from the top, with the page number 31 at its start.

**Visuals**

Photograph: a low-angle view looking up at a modern office building. In the left and centre a green cladding corner carries a large white TD logo on a sloped sign panel, with green panels below and a grey-and-white glass facade above. The upper-left corner shows a pale blue sky with white cumulus clouds. Lighting is bright daylight from the upper left, with soft shadows on the green panels. Dominant colours are green #1F8A3A, dark green #0E5A2A, grey #8C9196, and sky blue-white #D9E6EE. The image is cropped at its right edge at 60.1% of the slide width and runs full-bleed to the top, bottom and left edges. There is no overlay or tint. Logo: the TD square logo at top-right, a white TD wordmark on a green square. Three chevron icons: thin right-pointing chevron strokes, one at the left of each menu bar.

**Design**

Background is white #FFFFFF on the right and a full-height photograph on the left. Title 'TD Bank Group' is Arial Bold in green #008A00. Subtitle 'Key Themes' is Arial Regular in near-black #1C1C1C. Menu items use Arial Regular in light green #B1D57F for the first two items and Arial Bold in dark green #03522C for the active third item. Accent colours are light green panel #E5F1D4, chevron light green #B1D57F, chevron dark green #03522C, and TD green #00B200. Title text is about 5.2% of slide height and menu text about 4.4%. Footer text is Arial Regular, about 1.5%, in white #FFFFFF, all capitals for the tagline. Panels are flat rectangles with square corners, no border, no shadow and no gradient. Chevrons have thin strokes about 0.2% of slide height wide.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white slide: the left column (about 3.7% to 49% of width) holds the title block and bullets, and the right column (about 52% to 98%) holds two stacked charts, separated by a thin vertical divider at about 50% of width. The title Capital with superscript 1 sits top-left at about y 10.7%, with the subtitle beneath it at y 18.2%; the TD logo sits top-right at about x 92.6%, y 3.9%. The footer page number 32 and the footer text sit bottom-left at y 93.3%; there is no slide number other than the printed 32.

**Visuals**

Two waterfall charts and one legend. Chart 1 is a waterfall titled QoQ CET1 ratio with bars for Q4 2025 at 14.7% (dark green, about 14.7 units on a percent scale), Net Income - Adjusted² +67 bps (bright green floating bar), Dividends (31) bps (pale grey floating bar), RWA Growth (ex. Model / Methodology and FX) (18) bps (pale grey), Items of Note (3) bps (pale grey), Share Repurchase (38) bps (pale grey), Unrealized gains on FVOCI securities 4 bps (bright green), Model / Methodology Updates 3 bps (bright green), Other 1 bp (pale grey), and Q1 2026 14.5% (dark green). A dashed dark green rectangle encloses the first four bars and the label +18 bps and Adj² Organic Capital Accretion. No axis scale is printed. Chart 2 is a stacked waterfall titled QoQ RWA ($B) with superscript 3: Q4 2025 total 636.4 stacked as Market Risk 20.4 (light green cap), Op Risk 129.6 (bright green), Credit Risk 486.4 (dark green); steps Credit Risk - Asset Size and Other 3.8 (grey), Op Risk 0.3 (grey), Market Risk 3.5 (grey), Credit Risk - Asset Quality 0.7 (grey), Credit Risk - Model / Methodology Updates (1.4) (thin bright green), FX (8.0) (bright green); Q1 2026 total 635.2 stacked as Market Risk 23.9, Op Risk 129.9, Credit Risk 481.4. No axis scale is printed. A dashed dark green rectangle encloses the four middle steps with the text 8.2 or -18 bps and Total RWA growth ex. Model / Methodology and FX. Legend below chart 2 lists Credit Risk (dark green square), Op Risk (bright green square) and Market Risk (light green square). The TD logo is a solid green square with white TD letters.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with a green corporate palette. Titles and the subtitle are bold green #008A00; section headers and body bullets are dark green #03522C; chart headings are dark green #1A5336; accents are bright green #00B624 and #3AFF61, with dark green #215C33 and #163D22 for total bars. Typeface is Arial (sans-serif), bold for titles and headers, regular for body, chart labels and category labels. Title is about 5.2% of slide height, subtitle 3.7%, section headers and bullets 2.2 to 2.6%, chart values 1.7%, category labels 1.4%, footer 1.5%. Bullets are dark green square glyphs (Wingdings). Dashed rectangles use a dark green dashed stroke with square corners. Bars have no shadows or gradients.

Capital1
Non-CET1 Capital Management
TD’s capital instruments are diversified by structure, market, and currency, contributing to the resilience and stability of its capital position.
Canadian D-SIBs were required to meet their regulatory TLAC requirements by November 1, 2021.
As of Q1 2026, TD's risk-based and leverage-based TLAC ratios both exceed the regulatory minimum.

**Layout**

Title block top-left: 'Capital' with superscript 1 at about 3.7% from the left and 10.7% from the top, subtitle 'Non-CET1 Capital Management' below at y 18.2%. Left column holds three bullets at x 5.5 to 46%, y 29 to 50%. Right column holds the Risk-Based TLAC waterfall under a dark green header bar (y 18.8 to 22.5%) and the Leverage-Based TLAC waterfall under a header bar (y 58.9 to 62.6%). The bottom-left row holds the AT1 and T2 pie charts, each under its own header bar at y 58.7 to 62.4%. The TD logo sits top-right at x 95%, y 3.9%. Footer page number 33 and the text '— TD INVESTOR PRESENTATION Q1 2026' sit bottom-left at y 92.7%, left margin about 3.7%.

**Visuals**

Chart 1, Risk-Based TLAC Ratio waterfall (column chart, top right). Categories left to right: CET1, Additional Tier 1, Tier 2, Senior Debt (footnote 4). CET1 is a grey (#F2F2F2) column labelled 14.5%, with its top at about 14.5% of the stack height; Additional Tier 1 is a pale green (#E5F1D4) floating bar labelled 1.7%; Tier 2 is a bright green (#00B624) floating bar labelled 1.9%; Senior Debt is a light green (#AED99E) column labelled 13.0%. Dashed reference lines: Current Risk-based TLAC Ratio 31.1% (footnote 3) at the top, Published Minimum Risk-based TLAC Ratio 25.0%, and Published Minimum CET1 Ratio 11.5%. No axis scale numbers are printed; the base is a dark line. Chart 2, AT1 Breakdown pie (footnote 2). Five slices clockwise from top: CAD Retail Pref Shares $1.2B (10.3%) in light green #AED99E; CAD Institutional Pref Shares $1.7B (14.2%) in mid green #86CA72; SGD Perpetual Debt $0.3B (2.9%) in very pale green #E6F1E0; CAD LRCN $4.0B (34.5%) in bright green #00B624; USD LRCN $4.4B (38.1%) in dark green #163D22. Labels sit outside the pie in green text for the first three slices and inside in white for the two large slices. Chart 3, T2 Breakdown pie (footnote 2). Four slices: USD $3.4B (31.8%) in bright green #00B624 at upper left; EUR $1.2B (11.3%) in light green #AED99E; Other $0.3B (3.1%) in pale #E5F1D4 with a thin leader line; CAD $5.8B (53.8%) in dark green #163D22 occupying the lower half. Chart 4, Leverage-Based TLAC Ratio waterfall (bottom right). Categories: Tier 1, Tier 2, Senior Debt (footnote 4). Tier 1 is a light grey (#F2F2F2) column labelled 4.0% with a pale green (#E5F1D4) floating bar labelled 0.5% on top; Tier 2 is a bright green (#00B624) floating bar labelled 0.5%; Senior Debt is a light green (#AED99E) column labelled 3.6%. Dashed lines: Current TLAC Leverage Ratio 8.6% (footnote 3), Published Minimum TLAC Leverage Ratio 7.25%, Current Leverage Ratio 4.5%, Published Minimum Leverage Ratio 3.5%. No axis scale numbers are printed.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF with flat fills and no gradients, shadows or rounded corners. Titles 'Capital' and 'Non-CET1 Capital Management' in bold green #008A00. Section header bars are solid dark green #006700 with bold white (#FFFFFF) sans-serif titles. Body text in regular dark green #03522C. Chart labels in #03522C, with some labels in #1C1C1C and white on dark fills. Typefaces: Arial (Helvetica-like grotesque sans-serif) in bold for titles and headers, regular for body and labels, Wingdings bullet glyphs. Title 'Capital' about 5.2% of slide height; subtitle about 3.7%; section headers about 2.6%; body bullets about 2.2%; chart labels about 1.5 to 1.8%; footer about 1.5%. Pie and bar fills are flat with white separators between pie slices. Dashed reference lines are dark green #006700, thin. Axis baselines are near-black thin lines.
